== the script, run from the repository root as: uv run --locked python sweep.py
```python
"""Issue #15's spec: every float32 normalized value through the fixed map and the property.

The fourth run (after issue #13's `verification/sweep.txt` and the three of the revisions after PR
14's first and second reviews). The map's two inverses are fixed: `NormalizedScale.to_normalized`
steps to the float32 that lands on a preset where the nearest quotient misses it, and
`MappedParameter.to_preset` takes, where the curve's float32 is not one the scale holds, the
float32 next to it that the scale holds and that shows the display. This run counts, per group,
that the property's exact checks fail on no value but the one excuse's, and that the fixes change
a result only where today's was not one the scale holds. The pinned values are read from
tests/unit/test_parameters.py, the module whose tests hold them.

Counted for every scale/curve group of the committed map whose curve this arithmetic reproduces
bit for bit (linear, quadratic, quartic, square_root), for each of the six records with a curve
offset and each of the 12 envelope delay and hold records directly; HELD and NCHANGED alone for
the 9 exponential groups.

P0 = scale.to_preset(r), the preset the parameter holds; D0 = to_display(P0); P1 = the curve's
float32 for D0, clamped to the scale (rounded on an indexed scale), today's `to_preset` before the
fix; P1' = `to_preset(D0)` as shipped; N = the nearest float32 quotient, today's `to_normalized`
before the fix; N' = `scale.to_normalized` as shipped; S = scale.to_preset.
  SHARE     D0 is the display of the float32 just below or just above P0: `_shares_its_display`
  SHARE0    of those, values where A3 holds: the predicate true, never consulted
  A1        to_display(P1') != D0         the display back through the preset
  HELD      S(N'(P0)) != P0               the held preset back through the normalized path
  A2        S(N'(P1')) != P1'             the display's preset back through the normalized path
  SET       to_display(S(N'(P1'))) != D0  a set of the display shows it again
  A3        P1' != P0                     the preset back
  A3x       of those, not SHARE: an A3 failure `_shares_its_display` does not excuse
  CHANGED   P1' != P1                     the fix changed the display's preset
  CHANGEDx  of those, P1 one the scale holds: a change the fix must not make
  NONE      P1 not held, and no float32 next to it is held and shows D0
  BOTH      P1 not held, and both float32s next to it are held and show D0 (the tie-break)
  NCHANGED  N'(P0) != N(P0)               the fix changed the normalized value of a held preset
  NCHANGEDx of those, S(N(P0)) == P0: a change the fix must not make
`bound!` counts what breaks the property or the fix as shipped: A1, HELD, A2, SET, A3x, CHANGEDx,
NONE or NCHANGEDx.

The arithmetic is the map's, exactly: r * span is exact in a double (24 x 24 bits); a sum is
rounded once to float32 with TwoSum's remainder deciding a double that lands on a float32
midpoint; a quotient's nearest float32 is chosen from three candidates by exact distance, ties to
the even significand. A value whose subtraction this cannot certify exact is given to an exact
scalar quotient instead (`round_to_float32` of the `Fraction`s, today's formula), and counted
(`scalar`). Before counting, each group is checked against the shipped scalar methods on 200,007
values, every one of P0, D0, P1, P1', S(N'(P1')), N(P0), N'(P0) and S(N'(P0)), and SHARE against
the tests' own `_shares_its_display`; a group with a mismatch is not counted. An exponential group
is checked on P0, N(P0), N'(P0) and S(N'(P0)) alone.
"""

import math
import multiprocessing
import sys
import time
from fractions import Fraction

import numpy as np
from patchbay import ParameterId

from patchbay_vital.float32 import round_to_float32
from patchbay_vital.parameters import parameter_map

sys.path.insert(0, "tests/unit")
import test_parameters  # noqa: E402

REPRODUCED = ("linear", "quadratic", "quartic", "square_root")
PINNED = (*test_parameters._VOLUME_FLOOR, *test_parameters._POWERS_OF_TWO)
CHUNK = 1 << 22
SIGN = np.int64(0x7FFFFFFFFFFFFFFF)


def two_sum(a, b):
    s = a + b
    bp = s - a
    ap = s - bp
    return s, (a - ap) + (b - bp)


def round32(s, err):
    """Return the float32 nearest s + err exactly, s a double and err its exact remainder."""
    c32 = s.astype(np.float32)
    c = c32.astype(np.float64)
    diff = s - c
    toward = np.where(diff > 0, np.float32(np.inf), np.float32(-np.inf))
    nb = np.nextafter(c32, toward).astype(np.float64)
    away = (diff != 0) & (2 * diff == nb - c) & (err != 0) & (np.sign(err) == np.sign(diff))
    return np.where(away, nb, c)


def half_away(v):
    return np.copysign(np.floor(np.abs(v) + 0.5), v)


def ordered(x):
    """Return each double's place in value order as an int64, both zeros at 0."""
    bits = x.view(np.int64)
    return np.where(bits < 0, -(bits & SIGN), bits)


def nearest_quotient(scale, p):
    """Today's `to_normalized` before the fix: the float32 nearest (p - minimum) / span."""
    return round_to_float32((Fraction(p) - Fraction(scale.minimum)) / Fraction(scale.span))


class Vec:
    def __init__(self, rec, scale_only=False):
        s, c = rec.scale, rec.curve
        self.rec = rec
        self.mn, self.mx, self.span, self.ix = s.minimum, s.maximum, s.span, s.kind == "indexed"
        self.kind, self.mul, self.off = c.kind, c.multiply, c.offset
        assert scale_only or self.kind in REPRODUCED
        self.scalar = 0

    def S(self, r):
        s, err = two_sum(r * self.span, np.float64(self.mn))
        v = round32(s, err)
        return half_away(v) if self.ix else v

    def N(self, p):
        """The nearest float32 quotient, today's `to_normalized` before the fix."""
        a, ea = two_sum(p, np.float64(-self.mn))
        q = (a / self.span).astype(np.float32)
        best = None
        unsure = ea != 0
        for c32 in (np.nextafter(q, np.float32(-np.inf)), q, np.nextafter(q, np.float32(np.inf))):
            c = c32.astype(np.float64)
            d, ed = two_sum(c * self.span, -a)
            unsure |= ed != 0
            d = np.abs(d)
            even = (c32.view(np.uint32) & 1) == 0
            if best is None:
                best, bd, be = c, d, even
            else:
                take = (d < bd) | ((d == bd) & even & ~be)
                best, bd, be = np.where(take, c, best), np.where(take, d, bd), np.where(take, even, be)
        if unsure.any():
            index = np.flatnonzero(unsure)
            self.scalar += index.size
            best = best.copy()
            best[index] = [nearest_quotient(self.rec.scale, float(x)) for x in p[index]]
        return best

    def Nf(self, p):
        """`to_normalized` as shipped, with the scale's value there: (N'(p), S(N'(p)))."""
        q = self.N(p)
        s = self.S(q)
        miss = s != p
        if miss.any():
            i = np.flatnonzero(miss)
            toward = np.where(p[i] > s[i], np.float32(np.inf), np.float32(-np.inf))
            t = np.nextafter(q[i].astype(np.float32), toward).astype(np.float64)
            st = self.S(t)
            ok = (t >= 0) & (t <= 1) & (st == p[i])
            q, s = q.copy(), s.copy()
            q[i] = np.where(ok, t, q[i])
            s[i] = np.where(ok, st, s[i])
        return q, s

    def holds(self, p):
        q, s = self.Nf(p)
        return (q >= 0) & (q <= 1) & (s == p)

    def skew(self, p):
        if self.kind == "linear":
            return p
        if self.kind == "quadratic":
            return p * p
        if self.kind == "quartic":
            s = p * p
            return s * s
        return np.sqrt(np.maximum(p, 0.0))

    def unskew(self, y):
        if self.kind == "linear":
            return y
        if self.kind == "quadratic":
            return np.sqrt(np.maximum(y, 0.0))
        if self.kind == "quartic":
            return np.sqrt(np.maximum(np.sqrt(np.maximum(y, 0.0)), 0.0))
        return y * y

    def disp(self, p):
        return self.mul * self.skew(p) + self.off

    def inv(self, d):
        """Today's `to_preset` before the fix: the curve's float32, clamped, rounded if indexed."""
        v = self.unskew((d - self.off) / self.mul)
        p = np.minimum(np.maximum(v.astype(np.float32).astype(np.float64), self.mn), self.mx)
        return half_away(p) if self.ix else p

    def fixed(self, d0, p1):
        """`to_preset` as shipped: (P1', P1 held, NONE, BOTH)."""
        n = p1.size
        none, both = np.zeros(n, bool), np.zeros(n, bool)
        if self.ix:
            return p1, np.ones(n, bool), none, both
        held = self.holds(p1)
        out = p1.copy()
        i = np.flatnonzero(~held)
        if i.size:
            d, base = d0[i], p1[i]
            inverse = self.unskew((d - self.off) / self.mul)
            p32 = base.astype(np.float32)
            below = np.nextafter(p32, np.float32(-np.inf)).astype(np.float64)
            above = np.nextafter(p32, np.float32(np.inf)).astype(np.float64)
            ok_b = self.holds(below) & (self.disp(below) == d)
            ok_a = self.holds(above) & (self.disp(above) == d)
            below_first = np.abs(below - inverse) <= np.abs(above - inverse)
            first = np.where(below_first, below, above)
            second = np.where(below_first, above, below)
            ok_first = np.where(below_first, ok_b, ok_a)
            ok_second = np.where(below_first, ok_a, ok_b)
            out[i] = np.where(ok_first, first, np.where(ok_second, second, base))
            none[i] = ~ok_b & ~ok_a
            both[i] = ok_b & ok_a
        return out, held, none, both

    def paths(self, r):
        p0 = self.S(r)
        d0 = self.disp(p0)
        p1 = self.inv(d0)
        p1f, held1, none, both = self.fixed(d0, p1)
        p2 = self.Nf(p1f)[1]
        n0 = self.N(p0)
        nf0, s0 = self.Nf(p0)
        return p0, d0, p1, p1f, p2, n0, nf0, s0, held1, none, both

    def share(self, p0, d0):
        """Return where D0 is the display of the float32 just below or just above P0."""
        p32 = p0.astype(np.float32)
        below = np.nextafter(p32, np.float32(-np.inf)).astype(np.float64)
        above = np.nextafter(p32, np.float32(np.inf)).astype(np.float64)
        return (self.disp(below) == d0) | (self.disp(above) == d0)


def scalar_paths(rec, x):
    """The shipped scalar methods' P0, D0, P1, P1', S(N'(P1')), N(P0), N'(P0) and S(N'(P0))."""
    scale = rec.scale
    p0 = scale.to_preset(x)
    d = rec.from_normalized(x)
    p1 = min(max(rec.curve.to_preset(d), scale.minimum), scale.maximum)
    if scale.kind == "indexed":
        p1 = math.copysign(math.floor(abs(p1) + 0.5), p1)
    nf0 = scale.to_normalized(p0)
    return (
        p0,
        d,
        p1,
        rec.to_preset(d),
        scale.to_preset(rec.to_normalized(d)),
        nearest_quotient(scale, p0),
        nf0,
        scale.to_preset(nf0),
    )


def sample():
    rng = np.random.default_rng(15)
    bits = rng.integers(0, 0x3F800001, 200_000, dtype=np.uint32)
    named = np.array([*PINNED, 0.0, 1.0, 2.0**-149], dtype=np.float32)
    return np.concatenate([bits.view(np.float32), named]).astype(np.float64)


def check(name):
    """Return mismatches and scalar fallbacks of the vectorised paths against the scalar methods."""
    rec = parameter_map().find(ParameterId(name))
    v = Vec(rec)
    x = sample()
    p0, d0, p1, p1f, p2, n0, nf0, s0, *_ = v.paths(x)
    vector = (p0, d0, p1, p1f, p2, n0, nf0, s0)
    shares = v.share(p0, d0)
    bad = 0
    for i in range(x.size):
        try:
            scalar = scalar_paths(rec, float(x[i]))
            shared = test_parameters._shares_its_display(rec, scalar[0])
        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - a raise is a mismatch, counted
            bad += 1
            continue
        bad += (*scalar, shared) != (*(float(column[i]) for column in vector), bool(shares[i]))
    return x.size, bad, v.scalar


def check_scale(name):
    """Return mismatches of the vectorised P0, N(P0), N'(P0) and S(N'(P0)) against the scalar."""
    rec = parameter_map().find(ParameterId(name))
    v = Vec(rec, scale_only=True)
    x = sample()
    p0 = v.S(x)
    n0 = v.N(p0)
    nf0, s0 = v.Nf(p0)
    bad = 0
    for i in range(x.size):
        preset = rec.scale.to_preset(float(x[i]))
        back = rec.scale.to_normalized(preset)
        scalar = (preset, nearest_quotient(rec.scale, preset), back, rec.scale.to_preset(back))
        bad += scalar != (p0[i], n0[i], nf0[i], s0[i])
    return x.size, bad, v.scalar


SPANS = [("zero", 0, 1), ("sub", 1, 1 << 23)]
SPANS += [(str(e - 127), e << 23, (e + 1) << 23) for e in range(1, 127)]
SPANS += [("one", 0x3F800000, 0x3F800001)]
COLUMNS = (
    "SHARE", "SHARE0", "A1", "HELD", "A2", "SET", "A3", "A3x",
    "CHANGED", "CHANGEDx", "NONE", "BOTH", "NCHANGED", "NCHANGEDx", "bound!",
)
HELD_COLUMNS = ("HELD", "NCHANGED", "NCHANGEDx")


def count(name):
    """Count every float32 normalized value through one record's paths, by binade."""
    started = time.time()
    checked, mismatches, check_scalar = check(name)
    result = {"name": name, "checked": checked, "mismatches": mismatches,
              "check_scalar": check_scalar, "rows": [], "quiet": []}
    if mismatches:
        return result
    rec = parameter_map().find(ParameterId(name))
    v = Vec(rec)
    total = np.zeros(len(COLUMNS) + 1, dtype=np.int64)
    powers = set()
    for label, lo, hi in SPANS:
        row = np.zeros(len(COLUMNS) + 1, dtype=np.int64)
        for start in range(lo, hi, CHUNK):
            r = np.arange(start, min(hi, start + CHUNK), dtype=np.uint32)
            r = r.view(np.float32).astype(np.float64)
            p0, d0, p1, p1f, p2, n0, nf0, s0, held1, none, both = v.paths(r)
            shares = v.share(p0, d0)
            a1 = v.disp(p1f) != d0
            hd = s0 != p0
            a2 = p2 != p1f
            off = v.disp(p2) != d0
            a3 = p1f != p0
            a3_x = a3 & ~shares
            changed = p1f != p1
            changed_x = changed & held1
            nchanged = nf0 != n0
            nchanged_x = nchanged & (v.S(n0) == p0)
            bound = a1 | hd | a2 | off | a3_x | changed_x | none | nchanged_x
            share0 = shares & ~a3
            flags = (shares, share0, a1, hd, a2, off, a3, a3_x, changed, changed_x, none, both,
                     nchanged, nchanged_x, bound)
            row += [r.size, *(int(np.count_nonzero(f)) for f in flags)]
            if nchanged.any():
                powers.update((np.frexp(p0[nchanged])[1] - 1).tolist())
                result.setdefault("npow", True)
                result["npow"] &= bool(np.all(np.frexp(p0[nchanged])[0] == 0.5))
        total += row
        if row[1:].any():
            result["rows"].append((label, row.tolist()))
        else:
            result["quiet"].append(label)
    result["total"] = total.tolist()
    result["powers"] = sorted(powers)
    result["scalar"] = v.scalar
    result["seconds"] = time.time() - started
    return result


def count_held(name):
    """Count HELD and NCHANGED on every float32 normalized value of an exponential group."""
    started = time.time()
    checked, mismatches, check_scalar = check_scale(name)
    result = {"name": name, "checked": checked, "mismatches": mismatches,
              "check_scalar": check_scalar, "rows": [], "quiet": []}
    if mismatches:
        return result
    rec = parameter_map().find(ParameterId(name))
    v = Vec(rec, scale_only=True)
    total = np.zeros(len(HELD_COLUMNS) + 1, dtype=np.int64)
    for label, lo, hi in SPANS:
        row = np.zeros(len(HELD_COLUMNS) + 1, dtype=np.int64)
        for start in range(lo, hi, CHUNK):
            r = np.arange(start, min(hi, start + CHUNK), dtype=np.uint32)
            r = r.view(np.float32).astype(np.float64)
            p0 = v.S(r)
            n0 = v.N(p0)
            nf0, s0 = v.Nf(p0)
            nchanged = nf0 != n0
            flags = (s0 != p0, nchanged, nchanged & (v.S(n0) == p0))
            row += [r.size, *(int(np.count_nonzero(f)) for f in flags)]
        total += row
        if row[1:].any():
            result["rows"].append((label, row.tolist()))
        else:
            result["quiet"].append(label)
    result["total"] = total.tolist()
    result["scalar"] = v.scalar
    result["seconds"] = time.time() - started
    return result


def count_any(job):
    kind, name = job
    return (kind, count(name) if kind == "full" else count_held(name))


def group_key(rec):
    p, s, c = rec.parameter, rec.scale, rec.curve
    return (p.kind, s.kind, s.minimum, s.maximum, c.kind, c.multiply, c.offset, p.minimum, p.maximum)


def main():
    m = parameter_map()
    groups = {}
    for rec in m.parameters:
        groups.setdefault(group_key(rec), []).append(rec.id)
    counted = [(key, ids) for key, ids in groups.items() if key[4] in REPRODUCED]
    skipped = [(key, ids) for key, ids in groups.items() if key[4] not in REPRODUCED]
    offset = [rec.id for rec in m.parameters if rec.curve.offset != 0]
    envelope = groups[group_key(m.find(ParameterId("env_1_delay")))]
    print(f"== the committed map: {len(m.parameters)} records in {len(groups)} groups, keyed by")
    print("   (parameter kind, scale kind, scale min, scale max, curve kind, multiply, offset,")
    print("    parameter min, parameter max): every method the paths call reads these alone")
    print(f"-- counted, {len(counted)} groups ({', '.join(REPRODUCED)}), each by its first id:")
    for key, ids in counted:
        more = f" and {len(ids) - 1} more" if len(ids) > 1 else ""
        print(f"  {ids[0]}{more}: {key}")
    print(f"-- HELD and NCHANGED alone, {len(skipped)} groups: the skew is the platform's pow or")
    print("   log2, which numpy's exp2 and log2 do not reproduce bit for bit, so A1, A2, A3, SET,")
    print("   CHANGED, NONE and BOTH are not counted here; HELD and NCHANGED read the scale alone")
    for key, ids in skipped:
        more = f" and {len(ids) - 1} more" if len(ids) > 1 else ""
        print(f"  {ids[0]}{more}: {key}")
    print(f"-- the six records with a curve offset, each counted directly: {offset}")
    print(f"-- the {len(envelope)} records of env_1_delay's group, where HELD failed before the fix,")
    print(f"   each counted directly: {envelope}")
    names = [ids[0] for _, ids in counted]
    names += [name for name in (*offset, *envelope) if name not in names]
    held_names = [ids[0] for _, ids in skipped]

    started = time.time()
    jobs = [("full", name) for name in names] + [("held", name) for name in held_names]
    with multiprocessing.Pool(10) as pool:
        done = list(pool.imap_unordered(count_any, jobs))
    results = {r["name"]: r for kind, r in done if kind == "full"}
    held_results = {r["name"]: r for kind, r in done if kind == "held"}
    print(f"\n== {len(jobs)} counts in {time.time() - started:.0f} s on 10 processes")

    print("\n== the check against the shipped scalar methods, per count, before counting")
    print("   (200,000 bit patterns uniform over [0, 1], the pinned values of")
    print("    tests/unit/test_parameters.py, 0, 1 and 2 ** -149; P0, D0, P1, P1', S(N'(P1')),")
    print("    N(P0), N'(P0), S(N'(P0)) and SHARE against test_parameters._shares_its_display each")
    print("    compared; an exponential group's P0, N(P0), N'(P0) and S(N'(P0)) alone)")
    for name in names:
        r = results[name]
        print(f"  {name}: {r['checked']} values, mismatches {r['mismatches']},"
              f" scalar fallbacks {r['check_scalar']}")
    for name in held_names:
        r = held_results[name]
        print(f"  {name} (HELD and NCHANGED alone): {r['checked']} values, mismatches"
              f" {r['mismatches']}, scalar fallbacks {r['check_scalar']}")

    print("\n== every float32 normalized value, 1,065,353,217 a count, by its exponent")
    print("  binade: the float32 exponent of r ('sub' subnormal); then each count, as the docstring")
    print("  names them; bound!: the property or the fix broken")
    head = f"{'binade':>6} {'n':>10} " + " ".join(f"{c:>9}" for c in COLUMNS)
    grand = np.zeros(len(COLUMNS) + 1, dtype=np.int64)
    for name in names:
        r = results[name]
        rec = m.find(ParameterId(name))
        print(f"\n-- {name}: scale {rec.scale.kind} [{rec.scale.minimum!r}, {rec.scale.maximum!r}],"
              f" curve {rec.curve.kind} x{rec.curve.multiply!r} {rec.curve.offset:+},"
              f" parameter [{rec.parameter.minimum!r}, {rec.parameter.maximum!r}]")
        if r["mismatches"]:
            print("  NOT COUNTED: the check found a mismatch")
            continue
        print(head)
        for label, row in r["rows"]:
            print(f"{label:>6} {row[0]:>10} " + " ".join(f"{x:>9}" for x in row[1:]))
        t = r["total"]
        print(f"{'total':>6} {t[0]:>10} " + " ".join(f"{x:>9}" for x in t[1:]))
        grand += t
        quiet = r["quiet"]
        print(f"  nothing but n counted in the {len(quiet)} binades not printed;"
              f" scalar fallbacks {r['scalar']}; {r['seconds']:.0f} s")

    print("\n== per count, where anything is counted: the one excuse against its failures (A3x 0")
    print("   means every A3 failure has SHARE's shape), what each fix changed and whether it")
    print("   changed anything it must not (CHANGEDx, NCHANGEDx), and the tie-break (BOTH)")
    for name in names:
        r = results[name]
        t = r.get("total")
        if not t:
            continue
        named = dict(zip(("n", *COLUMNS), t, strict=True))
        if any(named[c] for c in ("SHARE", "A3", "CHANGED", "NONE", "BOTH", "NCHANGED")):
            powers = r.get("powers", [])
            span = (f"; NCHANGED at powers of two only: {r.get('npow')}, 2 ** {powers[0]} to"
                    f" 2 ** {powers[-1]}, {len(powers)}") if powers else ""
            print(f"  {name}: A3 {named['A3']}, unexcused {named['A3x']}; SHARE {named['SHARE']},"
                  f" never consulted {named['SHARE0']}; CHANGED {named['CHANGED']}, of a held P1"
                  f" {named['CHANGEDx']}; NONE {named['NONE']}; BOTH {named['BOTH']};"
                  f" NCHANGED {named['NCHANGED']}, of a landing N(P0) {named['NCHANGEDx']}{span}")

    print("\n== HELD and NCHANGED alone on the exponential groups, every float32 normalized value")
    for name in held_names:
        r = held_results[name]
        rec = m.find(ParameterId(name))
        if r["mismatches"]:
            print(f"  {name}: NOT COUNTED: the check found a mismatch")
            continue
        t = r["total"]
        print(f"  {name}: scale {rec.scale.kind} [{rec.scale.minimum!r}, {rec.scale.maximum!r}],"
              f" curve {rec.curve.kind}: n {t[0]}, HELD {t[1]}, NCHANGED {t[2]}, NCHANGEDx {t[3]};"
              f" scalar fallbacks {r['scalar']}; {r['seconds']:.0f} s")
        for label, row in r["rows"]:
            print(f"    {label:>6} {row}")

    print("\n== summary over every count")
    for column, value in zip(("n", *COLUMNS), grand, strict=True):
        print(f"  {column}: {value}")

    volume = m.find(ParameterId("volume"))
    print("\n== volume's pinned values at or below its floor (test_parameters._VOLUME_FLOOR)")
    print(f"  N'(2 ** -43) = {volume.scale.to_normalized(2.0**-43)!r}")
    for r in test_parameters._VOLUME_FLOOR:
        p0 = volume.scale.to_preset(r)
        d0 = volume.from_normalized(r)
        own = volume.curve.to_preset(d0)
        p1f = volume.to_preset(d0)
        p2 = volume.scale.to_preset(volume.to_normalized(d0))
        print(f"  r = {r!r} ({np.float32(r).view(np.uint32):08x}): P0 {p0!r}, D0 {d0!r},"
              f" the curve's float32 {own!r} (held {volume.scale.holds(own)}), P1' {p1f!r}"
              f" (held {volume.scale.holds(p1f)}), S(N'(P1')) {p2!r}, its display the one set"
              f" {volume.to_display(p2) == d0}, P1' == P0 {p1f == p0}")
    print("\n== the pinned powers of two (test_parameters._POWERS_OF_TWO), on env_1_delay")
    delay = m.find(ParameterId("env_1_delay"))
    for r in test_parameters._POWERS_OF_TWO:
        p0 = delay.scale.to_preset(r)
        n0 = nearest_quotient(delay.scale, p0)
        nf0 = delay.scale.to_normalized(p0)
        print(f"  r = {r!r} ({np.float32(r).view(np.uint32):08x}): P0 {p0!r}, N(P0) {n0!r}"
              f" ({np.float32(n0).view(np.uint32):08x}) holding {delay.scale.to_preset(n0)!r},"
              f" N'(P0) {nf0!r} ({np.float32(nf0).view(np.uint32):08x}) holding"
              f" {delay.scale.to_preset(nf0)!r}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

== the script, run from the repository root beside sweep.py (above) as:
   uv run --locked python sweep_exponential.py
```python
"""Issue #15's spec: the 9 exponential groups' curve claims, every float32 normalized value.

`sweep.py` counts these groups' scale alone (HELD, NCHANGED), since numpy's exp2 and log2 are not
the platform's `pow` and `log2`, which the shipped curve calls. This count calls the platform's
own, `math.pow` and `math.log2`, value by value, exactly as `DisplayCurve` does, and takes every
other step from `sweep.py`'s arithmetic: so it counts on these groups every column `sweep.py`
counts on the others, with the same definitions and the same check against the shipped scalar
methods on 200,007 values first. Run from the repository root beside `sweep.py`.
"""

import math
import multiprocessing
import sys
import time

import numpy as np
from patchbay import ParameterId

import sweep
from patchbay_vital.parameters import parameter_map

_Vec = sweep.Vec
_pow, _log2 = math.pow, math.log2


def _power_of_two(values):
    """`math.pow(2.0, x)` for each value, infinity where a double cannot hold it, as shipped."""
    listed = values.tolist()
    try:
        return np.array([_pow(2.0, x) for x in listed], dtype=np.float64)
    except OverflowError:
        out = []
        for x in listed:
            try:
                out.append(_pow(2.0, x))
            except OverflowError:
                out.append(math.inf)
        return np.array(out, dtype=np.float64)


def _logarithm(values):
    """`math.log2(y)` for each positive value, minus infinity otherwise, as shipped."""
    return np.array([_log2(y) if y > 0 else -math.inf for y in values.tolist()], dtype=np.float64)


class LibmVec(_Vec):
    """`sweep.Vec` with the exponential skews through the platform's libm, value by value."""

    def __init__(self, rec, scale_only=False):
        super().__init__(rec, scale_only=True)
        assert self.kind in ("exponential", "exponential_inverted")

    def skew(self, p):
        return _power_of_two(p if self.kind == "exponential" else -p)

    def unskew(self, y):
        logged = _logarithm(y)
        return logged if self.kind == "exponential" else -logged


sweep.Vec = LibmVec  # sweep's check and count build their Vec through this name


def group_key(rec):
    return sweep.group_key(rec)


def main():
    m = parameter_map()
    groups = {}
    for rec in m.parameters:
        groups.setdefault(group_key(rec), []).append(rec.id)
    names = [ids[0] for key, ids in groups.items() if key[4] not in sweep.REPRODUCED]
    print(f"== the {len(names)} exponential groups, each by its first id, every column of sweep.py")
    for key, ids in groups.items():
        if key[4] not in sweep.REPRODUCED:
            more = f" and {len(ids) - 1} more" if len(ids) > 1 else ""
            print(f"  {ids[0]}{more}: {key}")
    started = time.time()
    with multiprocessing.Pool(len(names)) as pool:
        done = {r["name"]: r for r in pool.imap_unordered(sweep.count, names)}
    print(f"\n== {len(names)} counts in {time.time() - started:.0f} s on {len(names)} processes")
    print("\n== the check against the shipped scalar methods, per count, before counting")
    for name in names:
        r = done[name]
        print(f"  {name}: {r['checked']} values, mismatches {r['mismatches']},"
              f" scalar fallbacks {r['check_scalar']}")
    head = f"{'binade':>6} {'n':>10} " + " ".join(f"{c:>9}" for c in sweep.COLUMNS)
    grand = np.zeros(len(sweep.COLUMNS) + 1, dtype=np.int64)
    print("\n== every float32 normalized value, 1,065,353,217 a count, by its exponent")
    for name in names:
        r = done[name]
        rec = m.find(ParameterId(name))
        print(f"\n-- {name}: scale {rec.scale.kind} [{rec.scale.minimum!r}, {rec.scale.maximum!r}],"
              f" curve {rec.curve.kind} x{rec.curve.multiply!r} {rec.curve.offset:+},"
              f" parameter [{rec.parameter.minimum!r}, {rec.parameter.maximum!r}]")
        if r["mismatches"]:
            print("  NOT COUNTED: the check found a mismatch")
            continue
        print(head)
        for label, row in r["rows"]:
            print(f"{label:>6} {row[0]:>10} " + " ".join(f"{x:>9}" for x in row[1:]))
        t = r["total"]
        print(f"{'total':>6} {t[0]:>10} " + " ".join(f"{x:>9}" for x in t[1:]))
        grand += t
        print(f"  nothing but n counted in the {len(r['quiet'])} binades not printed;"
              f" scalar fallbacks {r['scalar']}; {r['seconds']:.0f} s")
    print("\n== summary over the exponential counts")
    for column, value in zip(("n", *sweep.COLUMNS), grand, strict=True):
        print(f"  {column}: {value}")


def timed(name, values):
    """Time one group's paths and columns on `values` normalized values, for the estimate."""
    rec = parameter_map().find(ParameterId(name))
    v = LibmVec(rec)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(7)
    started = time.time()
    done = 0
    while done < values:
        size = min(sweep.CHUNK, values - done)
        r = rng.integers(0, 0x3F800001, size, dtype=np.uint32).view(np.float32).astype(np.float64)
        p0, d0, p1, p1f, p2, n0, nf0, s0, held1, none, both = v.paths(r)
        v.share(p0, d0)
        v.disp(p1f)
        v.disp(p2)
        v.S(n0)
        done += size
    return time.time() - started


if __name__ == "__main__":
    if sys.argv[1:2] == ["--time"]:
        n = int(sys.argv[2])
        seconds = timed("lfo_1_smooth_time", n)
        print(f"lfo_1_smooth_time: {n} values in {seconds:.1f} s, {seconds / n * 1e9:.0f} ns a value")
    else:
        main()
```
